\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I got stuck in an infinite loop of dropping and picking up the apple, peach, and borpulus between the pantry and kitchen, failing to actually brew the potion. My mistake was not executing the recipe steps (pouring, stirring, adding) once I had the necessary items, and instead trying to hoard all ingredients in my inventory simultaneously, which is impossible due to the two{-}item limit. Next time, I will follow the recipe strictly: 1) Go to the storage room to get the borpulus. 2) Go to the pantry to get the apple, then immediately go to the kitchen and "put apple in cauldron". 3) Return to the pantry to get the peach, then go to the kitchen and "put peach in cauldron". 4) Go to the magic workshop to get the ice wand, then go to the kitchen and "wave ice wand on cauldron". 5) Go to the garden (or wherever the spider egg is, likely the garden or storage room based on typical logic, but I must check the garden first as I missed it) to get the spider egg, then go to the kitchen and "put spider egg in cauldron". 6) Finally, go to the garden to get the ectoplasm (or check the garden/outhouse for it), go to the kitchen, "pour ectoplasm into cauldron", and "stir with borpulus". I will process items one by one, dropping them into the cauldron immediately upon arrival in the kitchen to free up inventory space, rather than trying to carry them all at once. I must also ensure I visit the garden to find the ectoplasm and spider egg, which I completely ignored in the previous attempt.} \\
\\

\texttt{New plan: My previous failure stemmed from a critical sequencing error and a misunderstanding of the game's state logic. The recipe explicitly requires pouring ectoplasm first to create the liquid base. Additionally, I wasted turns trying to retrieve the ice wand from the kitchen floor after dropping it, failing to realize I could simply retrieve it from the Magic Workshop (where it is on the ceiling) or that I needed to manage my inventory differently to hold the borpulus and ectoplasm simultaneously.} \\

\texttt{2.  **Retrieve Borpulus:** Go to the Storage Room (via Hallway) and take the "borpulus". (Inventory: Ectoplasm, Borpulus).} \\
\texttt{3.  **Start Potion:** Go to the Kitchen. "Pour bucket of ectoplasm into cauldron".} \\
\texttt{4.  **Stir:** "Stir cauldron with borpulus". (This should now work as the cauldron contains liquid).} \\
\texttt{5.  **Add Apple:** Go to the Pantry, take "apple", return to Kitchen, "put apple in cauldron".} \\
\texttt{6.  **Add Peach:** Go to the Pantry, take "peach", return to Kitchen, "put peach in cauldron".} \\
\texttt{7.  **Retrieve Ice Wand:** Go to the Magic Workshop (via Hallway), take "ice wand" (from ceiling), return to Kitchen.} \\
\texttt{8.  **Wave Wand:** "Wave ice wand on cauldron".} \\
\texttt{9.  **Retrieve Spider Egg:** Go to the Cellar (via Storage Room), take "spider egg", return to Kitchen, "put spider egg in cauldron".} \\
\texttt{10. **Finish:** Type "> done".} \\
